T-174, 2026-11-02: the main eggs' perk rewards, raised at the owner's request ("make sure the rewards are good, upgrade all current perks to max level for map 2 easter egg reward"). Requiem (Kaltenbrunn) already granted every tonic at tier I; it now also pours each one to its top tier (the new reward action `perks_max`, run right after `grant_all_perks`, with a banner), so the squad leaves the egg holding every tonic at tier IV, which is the permanent tier (T-129). Letzte Fahrt (Totenhafen) was the weakest main reward (2,500 Scrip and one tonic one tier up): it now pays 7,500 Scrip and raises every held tonic one tier (`tier_all`). Both go through PerkManager.raise_all_tiers, which caps each tonic at the highest tier it has data for (top_tier) and changes nothing under Classic Rules, which keeps tiers off. The Inn's reward (every tonic) and Fernflug's finale rewards are unchanged. Test: test_egg_rewards (3).

T-173, 2026-11-02: Totenhafen graphics pass (UPDATE 10 send-back; the owner: "there are some graphical bugs especially map 3"). An art pass: map02.tscn regenerated from gen_map02.gd, its 22 navigation meshes, 358 collision boxes and 20 NavigationLink3D blocks byte-identical to master's; no timing, prompt or hitbox moved. Found in the u10_after captures and the u10_blind clip, each with its cause and fix. (1) Pale green-white glowing lines on every pier, apron and catwalk edge, the door jambs and the stair nosings: the T-145 readability strips in lumi_paint (0.55 emission on a near-white albedo), 3.5 cm proud of the floor, bloomed into neon outlines in the dark hall and read as a debug draw; the pump stair's strip (an X-direction run that climbs from the catwalk to the tower) was never rolled to the stair, so it floated level at mid-height. Fix: a Map-2 key m2_lumi (a pale paint, 0.12 emission), the floor strips 1.6 cm high, _lumi_line rolls X runs to follow their slope (lumi_paint itself and Kaltenbrunn untouched). (2) The flooded water as a flat black slab: the sea shader had only vertex waves and dark deep/shallow colours, so under the pen's concrete roof there was nothing to reflect, and the spring sheets were 4-vertex PlaneMeshes (no waves at all) sharing the dock material. Fix: SEA_SHADER gets per-pixel world-space ripples (four travelling sines into the normal) and a `sheen` emission at grazing angles standing in for the hall's haze (works the same in Forward+ and Compatibility: no screen or depth texture), slightly lifted colours; TideController gives the sheets their own calmer copy (amp 0.06, no drain offset) on a 1 m-subdivided plane. (3) The large white flattened discs: the quay's two corner snow drifts, single 3 x 2.4 m white ellipsoids standing free of the walls in a wet-concrete quay; now low grey slush heaps (snow_trodden, three overlapping per corner) pressed into the corners, and the parapet and hut drifts lowered the same way. The searchlight's visible shaft was the other big grey shape: its CylinderMesh was rotated with the wide end (4 m radius) at the lamp, an unshaded flat add cone cutting through the tower walls; now the narrow end is at the lens and it uses the shared light_beam shader (fades along its length, at its silhouette and near the camera). (4) U-1047's hull washed red: the red battle lanterns dim to 15% on the power (T-154), but ZombieDirector's special round snapshots every light's energy at its start and restores the snapshot at its end, so the first special round after the power put the lanterns back at full red for the rest of the match (the clip's interior frames). Fix: _uboat_white stores the dimmed value as the lanterns' meta "energy" (what the director restores and flickers from while the power is on) and runs at setup when the power is already on (a joiner, a campaign arrival); the lanterns are also lower (1.15/0.8 to 0.8/0.55, ranges 8/7 to 7/6) so a hull wall 2 m from one is red, not saturated. (5) The cannery's harsh black-and-white checker: not a missing texture but tile_floor (two-tone terrazzo with a hard threshold) under the pendants; the cannery and the signal room now use m2_quarry, red-brown quarry tiles with soft tone steps. Also checked: no magenta or missing material in the regen log or the 35 stills, no Compatibility-only material in the map (the water and the shaft use plain spatial shaders); the black U-boat holds and the dark NW hall corner are lighting by design (T-145). Stills: morning/screenshots/u10_gfx/before|after/u09_map02 and u10_map02 (git-ignored; WSL + Xvfb, Compatibility; Forward+ not rendered here). Tests: smoke_map02_world checks the lanterns stay dimmed after a special round.

T-173 (cont.), Letzte Fahrt discoverability (the owner never found the egg). A plain Totenhafen match (not the launcher's forced one, not campaign) already loads map02_letzte_fahrt with s0a_load (a coffin on the crane hook) live from round 1, coffins and hook usable (smoke_lf_start); the gap was any pointer past the power. Requiem's pattern (things that hum or crackle, a voice naming the next place, the pause menu's current line) and Act I's SightNote are copied: LetzteFahrtGuide (gravelight/world/map02/letzte_fahrt_guide.gd, attached by LetzteFahrtWorld) plays Operator pointers OPR_LF_HINT_* (category M2_LF_HINT, priority 3, baked offline with gen_vo.py's OneCore voice) 6 s into a round, once each and only while their step is active and untouched (the hook and the coffins at rounds 2 and 4, the radios and the sea stairs after the power, the log pages from round 5); an amber lamp pulses on the empty crane hook with the crane creaking until ballast_loaded; first-sight minimap notes (SightNote_LF) on the hook, the coffins, the sea stairs, each radio and the rotor machine; the quest's banners name the next step (QuestManager banner actions take an optional banner_s, 5.5 s here); objectives.json's Totenhafen line goes on to the radios and the rotor machine after the power. Presentation only: no step logic, timing or reward changed. Tests: test_letzte_fahrt_guide, smoke_lf_start.

T-175, 2026-11-02: the Crucible's room on every map (the owner: "improve quality of pack a punch rooms on all maps"; branch u10-10-crucible-rooms). One data-driven dresser instead of seven generator passes: `CrucibleRoom` (gravelight/world/crucible_room.gd), attached by MapRoot after the ambience and before the web profile, builds what data/gravelight/crucible_rooms.json lists for the map in the Crucible's own frame (origin the floor under it, local -Z its front): kits for feed pipes with flanges, hand-wheel valves and violet sight glasses, banded tanks with gauges and Marke sight strips, the remaking bench (a rifle half re-taught with a violet-veined part, tools, a vice, the Ledger's forms), ledger stacks, cables, chains, a tool rack, a grate dais, the Marke arc on the floor, a portal frame, notices in the game's own fiction (LEDGER FORM R-E 7, the KÜHLANLAGE crate the drawings came in, U-1047's Bordbefehl, the Kranich's Ladeliste, the test range's RECOVERED EQUIPMENT, Unit 001's log; no brand). Every primitive folds into one ArrayMesh per material (a few small-part materials aliased into neighbours), so a room is 12-18 meshes plus 2-4 Label3D notices, 1.2k-2k triangles, and 3 lights: a key spot on the machine (the room's one shadowed light; every zone stays <= 6, C4), a warm work light and the violet Marke light; plus the machine's own hum loop (crucible_hum, new, synthesized in gen_audio.py like the roar; louder near it, up 4 dB and a little sharper while it forges, near silent unpowered) and steam / spark bursts (CPUParticles3D, both renderers, halved on the web profile). Reactive, never in charge: GLBus.crucible_started vents steam and hisses (crucible_hiss, new), the Marke glow and the key ramp with the forge read from the Crucible's own phase and forge_seconds, crucible_ready throws sparks from the mouth and flares the glow, the take (weapon_acquired from &"crucible") and an expiry vent again; no timing, price, prompt, collider or hitbox of the Crucible changed. The props obey one rule, tested: a primitive taller than 6 cm that reaches below 2.05 m stands off the baked navmesh (wall strips, existing NavBlocks, the acts' new bays), nothing but a flat piece enters the approach box (the use box and BotBrain's stand point 1 m in front of it), and the stand point is on the navmesh; so no path to the machine or anywhere runs through a prop. Structural fixes, in the generators (regenerated; only the Crucible node, the new NavBlocks and the zone's navmesh differ): the Kranich's, Groom Lake's and Mondlicht's Crucibles were placed with yaw 90 and faced a wall 3-4 m away, showing their rooms their backs (their violet key lights lit the back plate); they now face their rooms (yaw -90). gen_map04_lib gives every act's Crucible a NavBlock (the bots planned through the machine), 1.6 m high because Recast does not fill a closed box (a 2.4 m block left a walkable island under its lid, gen_map00's NAV_SOLID_H note), plus a NavBlock bay behind it in Groom Lake and on the Moon that the room fills (tanks, the feed manifold, the bench; Mondlicht's chrome portal inside its lit alcove). Per room: Crossroads cellar (feed pipes down from the vault into the machine's shoulders, a Ledger rack and form by the installers' bench, the crate the drawings came in, a key lamp, the Marke arc round the feed grate), Kaltenbrunn Core Vault (Nachtglas tanks either side of the riveted plate feeding the machine overhead, chains off the lintel, a key lamp on a jib), Totenhafen U-1047 bow (through the dresser only; gen_map02 and map02.tscn untouched: air flask, fold-down bench and pressure lines on the hull, the captain's order, the Ledger form, a key lamp under the deckhead; hidden with ViewCells group E), Werk Thalbach Messbunker (was a bare box: air tanks piped across the wall, a fitter's bench and rack, the test-stand order, a ledger stack on the desk, dais and arc), the Kranich centre body (tie-down straps, oxygen bottles, a parts crate, the cargo list, feed lines from the frames), Groom Lake silo control room, the Moon's concourse. Tools: gravelight/tools_src/crucible_survey.tscn prints each room's floor plan from the scene (navmesh, colliders, the room's footprints, '!' where one stands on the navmesh), its lights and the room's draw stats; capture set u10_crucible (two cameras a map: the room from its door, the machine close). Left for others: Kaltenbrunn's Crucible still has no NavBlock (gen_map01, not touched here), and Totenhafen's NavBlock_Crucible is 2.4 m high (an island under its lid; for the gen_map02 owner). Also: test_map04c_data still counted the two fixed Groom Lake documents of T-148; T-171 placed six, so the suite was red on master; the count is updated.

T-172, 2026-11-03: the bots finish the eggs of Maps 2 and 3 (UPDATE 10 section 10, branch u10-10-bot-eggs; the owner: "make sure the bot can do the easter egg for maps two and 3"; numbers in docs/QA_REPORT_U10.md section 5). Bot behaviour only: no round, enemy health, spawn count, human price or gameplay timing moved (data: ee.json shop_break / together / hold_rally / start_wait_s / egg_gun / retry_after_s, combat.json hunt.ee_pause_after_s / force_after_s, actions.json buy's input buy_pressure, the Requiem's s1 bot_task priority 15 and s6 pause_pressure 0.8). **Shopping that buys:** the T-153 shop break bought nothing for four reasons, each fixed: the economy measured its ranges from the human 40-60 m away (a break now shops around the bot within 60 m, the Crucible too), the buy action's very_calm curve wanted pressure under 0.2, which Totenhafen's spawns never give (buy weighs buy_pressure = pressure x0.3 in a break), the follow-the-leader pull won every think (a shopping bot counts as roaming), and the break ended before the walk did (it runs on while the purchase errand walks, 50 s at most); breaks also come between two steps, before every hold (once per hold step, the starter and the joiners), drink Ironhide / Second Wind / Marathon first, may buy the doors on the way to the main egg's open steps, start only when something is on the list, never for the agent or under pressure, and back off x2-x4 when they bought nothing (one soak took 496). **The squad together:** while a bot holds (or walks to start, or waits at) a main-egg hold, every other free or Full Partner bot joins that step on the same anchor and never starts one of its own (BotEEHub hold rally; "near" then "hold"), and from round 6 the starter waits for every standing bot (45 s at most): master's runs split the diesel holds 40-60 m apart and the lone holder died, the rally held them together in every later run. From round 9 a Full Partner bot alone (no teammate within 24 m or on another storey) and under threat pauses its errand ("apart", 8 s at least) and picks no far step mid-round; a free bot escorts a Full Partner bot whose errand is far from everyone (escort_pid); a long-stalled round is the squad's (errands pause for the hunt after 45 s, and after 120 s the hunt takes a straggler the agent gave up as unreachable: a Foreman wedged at the Minehead held round 13 open for 130 game minutes). An "apart" rule on distance alone was tried and dropped: it stalled Totenhafen's cipher walk for 22 minutes. **Kaltenbrunn's steps:** the pylons start without the player after start_wait_s (30 s) before round 9 or in a break, with priority 15 (the vault was open by rounds 5-14, master's batch often never started s1); a designated egg-gun bot (the richest with a long gun) saves for and feeds the Crucible as soon as it can pay, no min round or tier, while a step needing a taught gun is still to come (s4 was done in every run); the scout goes before a collect step or a side quest, takes a collect bot's slot when every egg slot is full, and walks a grid per zone; the Four Bells are shot with a regular gun (a wonder never rang one), a target with no shot fired after 35 s of work gets another angle, a door under a bell 6-10 m up is found, and a step a bot just failed to reach waits 45 s for it. The agent's plan passes a perk it can never buy (Second Wind's retired machine, a full cap: the agent sat on 38,000 Scrip with an MP40). **Result: neither 3-of-5 target is met.** Totenhafen completed 2 of 7 runs (40.9 game min on the first T-172 code, and 28.0 game min, 16/16, the fastest so far, in the one run of the final code; master's last batches 1/3 and 0/3); the four failures in between were wipes in the x2 diesel holds at rounds 12-23 with the squad together but short of taught guns, which the final code's pre-hold break addresses. The owner's one-hour time box ended the batches, so the final code has 1 Totenhafen run, not 5. Kaltenbrunn completed 0 of 5 honest runs (master 0/5; the final code's one run, seed 31, did s0-s7 except s2 and wiped at round 41 after 106.6 game min), but two runs reached s7 (master's best s5): s6 and s7 by bots at rounds 11 and 13 in seed 33, and no squad wiped before round 22; what stops it now is s8a (the shard into the Crucible failed 165 times in seed 33; the final code logs the Crucible's phase), a calling radio never heard (s2a: the scout walks for seen needs only, so s2b and s8a stay locked), wedged stragglers holding a round open, and s6 under late-round threat. Tests: test_bot_u10_t172.gd (pure rules: perk_first, apart, the joiner plan, egg_gun_ok, the agent's never-buyable perk, the data blocks); the test contexts carry buy_pressure. Diagnostics: smoke_letzte_fahrt prints each bot's guns, perks and Scrip; ai_soak --egg-know=<keys> (tuning only).

T-176, 2026-11-06: the code review of T-172's merge (76078a70; the owner: "fix these all"; branch u10-11-review-fixes). Ten bugs in the bot egg code, all fixed, bot behaviour only (no round, price, prompt or gameplay timing moved; one data key added, combat.json hunt.ee_pause_max_s 120). **The hold rally:** a joiner standing in a charging hold now leads the rally when the lead goes down or out (the joiners were cancelled 6 s later and the charging hold failed empty, the very wipe the rally was for; BotEEHub._rally_lead_order: own holds first, then a joiner that holds_now); a joiner already on the lead's step follows the lead's anchor (its focus moves to the lead's node unless it is holding one itself: on the pylons it went to the first not-done anchor in list order while the lead started another alone); the starter's gather wait is per anchor (gather_t kept the first pylon's time, so pylon 2 onward started at once with nobody near), and the code defaults match ee.json (45 s, 3). **The egg's Crucible gun:** egg_gun_pid checks for a taught gun in the squad before it picks, so its 2 s cache can't keep a stale bot that then held back Scrip and walked 90 m to feed a second gun. **The hunt:** the hunt pause of a Full Partner errand lasts ee_pause_max_s at most (BotEE.hunt_pause_on: from 45 s to 165 s into the stall; the forced hunt's hunters can pace a fence for the rest of the round, and the errand was dropped and re-picked into the same pause), after which the round_end_max_s rule lets it resume; the forced hunt ranks a straggler it can reach above an unreachable one, then a boss (BotSquad.hunt_rank: it kept the boss, a Foreman wedged behind the fence, and ignored the walker that would end the round). **Doors:** the egg's route doors are no longer "ordered": they skip only the near-the-human rule, never before the human opened a door of their own (BotEconomy.egg_door_allowed; no human: the squad's call), keep the normal Scrip reserve, value 0.9, and the round's delay holds for every other door while one is listed (a non-empty list opened the gate for all of them); BotEE._door_on_way searches down to 11 m and with the loose tolerance only for a shot target (`deep`: the shoot vantage's door, route_doors for shoot_target steps), a walk goal keeps T-163's tight rule (a goal upstairs whose floor didn't snap took the floor 4.5 m under it and bought an unrelated door). **The scout:** a scout that walked the map and failed not_found rests every scout for retry_after_s (scout_job), and neither Full Partner's pick nor _scout_first gives the scout to a bot inside its own back-off (it restarted at once with a fresh visited set, looping the map, and kept cancelling the radio collectors). **Cost:** shop_wanted keeps its answer 2 s while the bot's Scrip is unchanged (a due break that lost to a higher pause, a teammate down or the round's end, rebuilt the economy's whole candidates list twice on every 5 Hz think), and the pre-hold break no longer probes twice in one tick. Tests: test_bot_u10_review.gd (egg_door_allowed, hunt_pause_on, hunt_rank, the gather defaults against ee.json); the bot suites (test_bot_ee, _shop_break, _u10, _u10_eggs, _u10_t172, _brain) unchanged and green.

T-177, 2026-11-06: the squad bots and the companions use the bank, buy tonics, the Crucible and weapon upgrades on their own (the owner: "for the extra bots and npcs you find make sure they can use the bank and buy perks and pap and upgrade their weapons"; branch u10-11-bots-shop). Who: the three squad bots (BotBrain + BotEconomy already bought tonics, wall guns, the crate and the Crucible; the Armorer's Bench only on a mark order, the bank never) and the companions, Jakub and the four map companions (Trudi, Gustl Ammer, Hinnerk, Jette), who earn Scrip with their own kills but had brain shopping / perks off and personality_jakub's buy weight 0 (T-051, T-127 "never of his own accord": reversed at the owner's word). Not the relief-squad soldiers (T-142): they pass through for 80 s with no Scrip of their own (their kills pay the nearest player), and the bot-mode agent never banks (its bank is the owner's profile). **The bank:** a bot or companion saves at the same counter in an account of its own for this match (BankAccount bot_*: the human's profile balance, and so the persistent savings, never move; the same interest at each round start, BankLedger; shut in Classic Rules; cleared by Economy.reset; a campaign hop pays it into the Scrip it carries, Campaign.serialize_player). Once a round, in a break, BankAccount.bot_plan: it banks all but its reserve + the price of the next thing it wants (its next buyable tonic, else a Crucible teaching it is up for, else the bench's pick; at least min_move 1000), and takes out what makes that next thing affordable, never a part payment (withdraw value 0.85, so it comes before the purchase it enables; a deposit is priority 0.3, after everything else). **The Armorer's Bench:** a "bench" shopping kind for the main gun (ArmorersBench.options_for_gun; the gun is selected first), the marks.json bench_prefs order (BotMarkOrders.pick_bench_option), in a break from round 3, after the reserve, the Crucible / egg hold-back and keep_scrip 1500, priority 0.6 (after tonics 0.95, guns 0.8), and one part per slot (BotEconomy.bench_rows_open: the first smoke run swapped radium and aperture sights back and forth for 600 / 750 a time). **Companions:** brain perks / shopping on (spawn kit and bonus perk stay off), personality buy 1.2, crate 0 (never the Requisition Crate), refuses_perks false; they shop only between rounds (mid-round they cover the one they follow), and in a calm break (pressure under 0.3) the escort's pull no longer grows with distance (it turned a shopping errand back at 6 m every time, and beside the player escort x1.5 + hysteresis beat the bench and the bank forever); with nothing to buy escort still wins and they walk back to the player. A bank errand re-checks its plan at the till (bot_plan on the pocket it has there: a deposit planned for 5,450 banked the 200 left after a purchase and used the round's visit). Map-mark orders still refuse a companion's purchase (the Talk card's tonic order is unchanged). Balance note: a companion with tonics is sturdier than before on top of its 0.6 damage_taken (that number assumed no perks). Also (the Map 1 perf check, T-178): a Full Partner bot whose planner hands back a node it just failed to reach (Requiem's s0 power switch behind a door it can't pay for) now ends the step "unreachable" (the 45 s back-off) instead of a full-map path query and a log line every physics tick. Tests: test_bots_shop_t177.gd (bot_plan, the bot accounts vs the profile, Classic Rules, one part per slot, the data), test_jakub / test_bot_brain updated to the new rule; smoke_bots_shop (Kaltenbrunn: 3 bots + Gustl, 30,000 Scrip each, a long break, widened ranges; tonics, bench parts, a deposit, and phase 2's withdrawal for a tonic; PASS) and smoke_bots_shop_m0 (Map 0 with Trudi: the Crucible too, every bot and the companion taught a gun; PASS). Kaltenbrunn's Crucible is in the Core Vault, which only the egg opens, so the Crucible is checked on Map 0.

T-182, 2026-11-06: the sights line up when aimed (the owner: "some of the guns, like the sights aren't actually lining up when you aim down sights, so that should be fixed a little bit"; branch u10-12-ads-sights). **Why the checks missed it.** T-047's ads_check proves the SightPoint / FrontSight markers sit on the screen centre, and they always did (324/324): the ADS pose is built from them. What was wrong was where the markers sat on the guns. T-128's picture heuristic took a rear sight's body for a front post and passed the rest. **The new measure** (`gravelight/tools_src/sight_picture.gd`, `SightPicture`): one ray per pixel round the aim point through the pure ADS pose (rig scale, compression, ADS FOV, 720 p, the camera's near plane), the nearest triangle of the gun and of the survivor's arms, split by depth into the rear sight (nearer than half way to the front marker) and the front sight; from that the front post's tip (a narrow column: a hood's or a ring's arch widens at once and is skipped), the post's centre, the rear notch's / aperture's opening round it, and "blocked" (the aim point on the rear sight, a receiver or a glove). Glass is see-through (the Panzerschreck window). **Found** (27 base guns; the Crucible looks and bench builds share them): Webley, MP40, StG44, BAR and MG42 had the rear marker 1.3-2.8 mm under the notch, so the rear sight hid the post and the shot went 3-9 px under the notch; the MP40, StG44, PPSh-41, G43 and BAR front markers sat on the front sight's hood top (the T-089 scan's "highest point"), the post 6-11 px below; the G43 picture was right but 9 px under the aim point; the Kar98k's and Thompson's sights sit 6.4 / 2.5 mm left of the bore line in those Sketchfab models (the Kar98k V 11 px left of the aim point, the Thompson's rear ears 9 px and blade 3 px left); the M97's receiver hid its bead; the Kar98k's and the Sawed-Off's support gloves covered the front sight / rib. 152 of the 258 iron-sighted builds failed, 0 after. Not the cause: the T-133 smooth-motion camera (the rig rides the eye; the holder animates after the camera each frame), FOV (the rig scale keeps the centre ray), arms per survivor (meshes only, the same IK), the shot point (the camera centre, spread only), sway (aimed idle drift 0.2-1.3 px; the Springfield's 3.3 px is under its HUD scope; left as T-047 tuned it). **Fixed at the source**, `data/gravelight/weapons/model_fit.json` only: sight_rear / sight_front for webley, kar98k, gewehr43, mp40, thompson, stg44, bar, ppsh41, mg42, trench_gun, sawed_off (fitted with the new `ads_check --autofit`, which moves the markers to the rear opening (an aperture's centre, a notch a quarter of its depth under the shoulders, so the post shows level with them) and the post's tip, re-aiming between steps; the Thompson keeps its rear height, as the line must clear the 1928's cocking knob (test_sight_clearance), so its blade tip stands level with the ear tops; the shotguns by hand: the eye 1.4-4.3 mm higher so the bead / rib clears the breech), left_hand 1.5-1.6 cm lower for kar98k and sawed_off. The bench parts follow the markers (radium dots, the AA ring, the bead, the scope heights moved with them). No weapons.json value, recoil, damage, timing, hitbox or hip pose changed (the support hand is lower at the hip too, visual only). **Kept fixed**: ads_check fails a build whose sight geometry is more than 2 px off the aim point (`--geo-tol`; `--no-geometry` for the old marker-only check; `--geometry` / `--geometry-map` print the measure and the class map with the mesh at the aim point, `--live` the drift sway and kick add, `--arms=` a survivor's hands), and the new GUT `test_ads_sight_geometry` checks every gun and Crucible look, every bench sights x stock build, every survivor's hands, the idle-sway drift (< 2 px), and that a marker 2.5 mm under a notch is caught. Scoped builds (66) keep the marker check: their picture is the HUD reticle. Shots: morning/screenshots/t182_before and t182_after (ads_check renders via WSL + Xvfb; git-ignored), contact sheets `_sheet_*.png` in each.

T-183, 2026-11-06: the squad panel, smaller perk caps, better tonic icons (branch u10-12-hud-squad; the owner: "we don't need to see the kills ... the scores all the time. Just have their names, whether or not they're down, and really small logos for what perks they have. The perks circles on the screen are a little bit too big ... make those icons just a little bit better"). The left-side squad panel (`coop_hud.gd`, now `CoopHud`, drawn by one Control instead of a Label per row) shows one row per teammate: co-op humans and bots by pid, then any companion with the squad (Jakub, Trudi, Gustl, Petersen, Jette: they were not on it before; a joined companion in a pure solo match now brings the panel too, `MapRoot.ensure_companion_session`). Your own row is gone (your caps are the HUD row). A row is a state mark, the name in the player's colour (companions a warm brown), a state word (DOWN 23 with the bleed-out clock and a pulsing red cross, REVIVING in gold while someone holds use, BLED OUT for a squad member who is back next round, OUT COLD for a companion who is gone for the match) and 16 px caps (`ui.json hud.squad_cap`, rows `squad_row_h` 24; the bonus perk first with its gold ring). No Scrip, kills or health bars (the nameplates over the bots still show health). The numbers moved to a scoreboard on the long-bound but unused `ui_scoreboard` action (hold Ctrl / Back): a ledger plate with everyone including you, Scrip, kills and 30 px caps with their tier bands; the pause LOADOUT tab's squad rows add the kills. The panel sits on the HUD grid under the minimap and below the debug lines when they show; five rows end near y 560 at 1080p, far above the perk row. The HUD's own perk caps went 54 -> 40 px (gap 10 -> 8; six caps 364 -> 280 px wide), still big enough for the band's numeral. The caps (`perk_cap.gd`, still the one renderer for the HUD, plates, panel, scoreboard, pause list and cabinet signs) got their own enamel per tonic in `PerkCap.ENAMEL` (perks.json "color" stays the cabinets' colour, and no data file was touched): the three pale blues, the two olives and the two golds read as one cap at small sizes, so Marathon went cobalt, Skyhook rosé, Magpie charcoal, Ledger the ledger's paper with red ink, Twin Barrel yellower, Blastproof redder (a unit test keeps every pair of enamels apart). Glyphs redrawn bolder and distinct in silhouette: a swallow from below against a crane in side flight and a magpie in profile with a gold coin; the mule faces you with long upright ears (it was the hare's twin); a boot with heel and toe; the moustache in ink on amber. Small caps (under 22 px) draw fewer, deeper crimp teeth, a 20% larger glyph without fine detail, a keyline round the face, a light crimp on a dark enamel, and the tier as a brass badge at the rim with one stroke per tier (the crimp is brass at II and gold at III, the permanent IV keeps its gold rim). The icons are procedural (convex polygons, circles, arcs, lines drawn at runtime), so there is no asset folder; `gravelight/tools_src/shots_squad_hud.tscn` renders the panel, the scoreboard and an icon contact sheet (every tonic at 16-76 px, every tier, dark and pale grounds; `--icons-only` for the sheet alone) under WSL + Xvfb (`tools/wsl_godot.sh`, which now takes XVFB_SCREEN for windows over 1080p). Shots in morning/screenshots/t183_before and t183_after (1080p, 21:9 and 1440p; Compatibility renderer, the browser profile; the Forward+ HUD is the same 2D canvas). Found on the way: a Control that calls `queue_redraw.call_deferred()` inside its own `_draw` crashed Godot 4.7.2 headless (signal 11), so the pulsing cross redraws from the per-frame `set_rows` instead. Tests: test_squad_panel.gd (rows without numbers, the four states, bonus-first caps, the scoreboard's numbers, the binding, the smaller caps, distinct enamels, small caps at every tier).

T-180, 2026-11-06: Totenhafen's glitched and unfinished spots (the owner: "on the third map ... especially the submarine, textures aren't loading ... it seems very incomplete ... doors and areas where there's invisible walls that you can't see"; branch u10-12-totenhafen-fix). Method first, so it can be re-run: MapArtAudit (gravelight/tools_src/map_art_audit.gd; tools_src/audit_map_art.tscn runs it on any map, `--probe` / `--ray` to dig into one spot) loads the map with every runtime dresser (MapRoot's ZoneAmbience, MapAmbience, CrucibleRoom, Totenhafen) and (1) walks every visible GeometryInstance3D for a mesh without surfaces, a surface without a material (flat white), a texture whose file is gone, a shader without code, an invisible or debug-magenta material and a UV-mapped material on a surface without UVs (the generator's batches drop UVs; surface_pbr's `triplanar` counts); (2) copies the visible art into trimesh shapes on spare physics layers, one per ViewCells group, and from points on the baked navmesh a survivor can walk to (paths from Player1's spawn, so the islands the bake leaves on a box's lid are not viewpoints) casts 160 rays each at body and eye height: a ray that stops on a world/door collider with no art at or before it and none within 0.3 m (0.45 m for Rail_* railings, whose posts and rails have gaps) is an invisible wall there, and art hidden by the viewer's cell with the next shown art 2 m+ behind is a hole you see through; (3) opens every BuyDoor and checks nothing is left in its opening. Found on master: 0 material faults (no null material, missing file or UV-less textured surface anywhere in the loaded map, incl. the runtime dressers); what read as textures that never loaded were four 2.2 m slabs of flat untextured black (the pre-T-094 chalk boards behind the hut's, the cutter's, the cannery's and the workshop's wall-buys, the cutter's standing free in front of its transom: removed, the wall-buys carry their own painted board), U-1047's see-through bulkheads and hold (below), and its hull wrapped in a flat plate wall; 41 of the map's own colliders reaching past their art and 7 see-through spots; the doors were clean. The worst, by rays hitting nothing: the 5.2 m railing boxes beside the two catwalk stairs (an invisible wall under each flight and 4 m over its handrail; their top landings had no railing art at all), the lower pens' pumps, the cutter on the quay (a 9 x 6 x 2.6 m box round a pointed hull that floated over the quay on nothing: its cradle was only in the round-15 remnant), the torpedo cradles, the tower ramp's 4 m rail (whose level railing art was buried inside the ramp), the conning tower box, and in U-1047 itself: the three watertight bulkheads were never drawn (only the oval door rims and two 25 cm strips at the hull: you looked straight through the wall beside the door you had to use), the companionway's 4 m rails (75 cm of nothing above the hatch coaming on the deck, nothing under the steps inside), a deckhead frame and the port pipe run crossing the hatch. The see-through spots: the railing along the north apron's edge in front of the bow was tagged with the hidden boat interior (its centre fell in the E box) so it vanished from the hall; the bow's narrowing hull let the interior's side plating (hidden from the hall) poke out, so from pier 1 you looked into the dark hold; the tower's south face was hidden from the pump stair. Fixes, all in gen_map02 / detail_map02 / map02_ambience.json / MapAmbience (map02 regenerated): props whose box is bigger than their art (brine tanks, can pallets, torpedo cradles, the lathe, the pumps with their flywheels, the gutting lines, the conveyor, a stove, the conning tower, the cutter) keep the box in the navmesh bake but turn it navmesh-only at runtime (group bake_only, MapRoot's T-125 switch) and get a fitted body: one convex hull per connected piece of their dressing inside the box (+0.3 m), so you bump into what you see and the gaps between pieces stay open; the baked navmesh is unchanged, so no zombie or bot path moved (21 of 22 navigation meshes byte-identical; E_UBOAT differs only by the island on NavBlock_Crucible's lid, now 1.6 m like the acts', T-175's open item). The cutter stands on its cribs and shores from the start and has two fitted bodies, hull+cradle until it slips at round 15, then remnant+cradle (map02_ambience.json cutter_slip hull_body / remnant_body; MapAmbience swaps their layers). NavBlocks under things that carry their own collider (the perk machines, the Crucible, the two crate spots, the bank's counter, the ammo boxes, the Armorer's Bench) are navmesh-only too: an empty crate spot is no longer an invisible box, the half metre round a machine no longer stops you short. The tall stair / ramp / companionway rails become a grating panel with a steel angle under the flight (and a collider of exactly that) plus a band along the handrail; the landings got their railing; the hatch a guard rail on deck up to the old collider top; the gangway's 2.2 m rails canvas screens on stanchions with a sloped handrail and a top rope. U-1047: bulkhead plating on both faces round an oval cut-out the size of the door rim, stiffeners, rivets and a brass builder's plate; the 1.6 m flat plate "bulwark" all round the deck (it read as a shipping container's wall, not a boat) is now a toe rail with stanchions and three guard wires up to the colliders' top; timber fenders, walings and a rope bow-pudding fill the 0.6 m between the bow and BowCap's face on the apron; type_vii gained `hold_to`: along the interior's length the hull's tanks and bilge enclose the 6 m x 3.5 m interior box (easing back to the Type VII lines over 5 m after it); the deckhead frames and the port pipe run stop at the hatch. ViewCells: the E box starts at z 38.05 (the apron railing is D again), the companionway ramp, its handrails and the bow plating draw with DH (seen from the hall and from inside), the lower pens' cell shows G. The white plank of snow along the cutter is grey slush (T-173's rule). No timing, prompt, spawn rule, door, weapon or enemy hitbox changed; the egg's objects are untouched (smoke_letzte_fahrt). Left: the perk machines' own collider (perk_machine.gd, 2.4 m tall over a cabinet whose top is rounded) and the Crucible's (crucible.gd) reach 20-30 cm past their art at the top: shared scripts, all maps, printed by the smoke but not failed; the U-boat's hull keeps its box-section gameplay deck (6 m wide to the bow) and its rusty_painted_metal plating reads streaky; in the Compatibility renderer the cannery is very dark (many power lights over large batches) and web_profile's dual-paraboloid omni shadows log "not supported" (Forward+ unaffected). Tests: smoke_map02_art (the audit with zero findings on the map's own colliders, materials and ViewCells, the doors, the fitted bodies on and their boxes off, the cutter's swap). Captures: morning/screenshots/t180_map02/before|after (WSL + Xvfb, Compatibility; capture set t180_map02).

T-179, 2026-11-06: Skyhook Sekt and Magpie Mead on Map 0, in a late-delivery bay off the Helvetia cellar (the owner: "there's two perks that aren't in any of the maps that I just can't seem to find. But when you do the Easter egg, you get them. Can you make sure you just expand the cellar area a little bit, add those perks, and ... just overall just make that experience better"; branch u10-12-cellar-perks). The two tonics' machines stood only on Fernflug's acts (map04b/c/d), while Kaltenbrunn's Requiem reward (T-174) hands out every tonic, so a player met them first as a reward. **Where:** a 6.2 x 7 m vault east of the cellar under the field (gen_map00 BAY_*: x 36..42.2, z 22.6..29.6, its roof the snow like the cellar's; GroundE is a slab with a hole over it now), through a 1.66 m arch in the cellar's east wall at z 25.12..26.78 (BAY_ARCH), the one gap in the east row wide enough without moving a machine (between Marathon Malt's and Hawkeye Brandy's footprints, short of the vault rib that lands at z 27). East, not south: south of the vault is under the stable's east wall and the yard fence; the field east is open (the parachute tree and the supply-drop ammo box stand on the bay's roof, unchanged). Nothing existing moved: the ten machines, the Crucible, the lever, the stairs, the breach, the coal-chute spawn; only the east wall's second shelf (dressing, z 26.1) gave way to the arch. **Layout:** both machines on the bay's far wall at x 40.3 facing the arch (yaw 90 like the east row; CELLAR_PERKS, so their nav blocks come with them; MACHINE_FOOTPRINTS gains glider and bandit), 1.75 m off the wall for the back-panel walk, 2.35 m apart; a 3.8 m open apron between the arch and their fronts; the two opened delivery crates are the only solids (baked, under NAV_SOLID_H, no_mantle), in the corners beside the arch. Zone CELLAR (a third Zone_CELLAR box), no door, no window, no spawn: the bay is a dead end and the map's cellar rule is the one coal-chute spawn, so the dead come in through the arch, as at the Crucible's alcove. **Power and light:** ordinary PerkMachines (needs_power, the cellar lever); a sixth CellarBulb over the apron in the lever's CELLAR sweep (range 5.5, so it doesn't reach back through the wall onto the east row) and an oil lantern by the arch that is always lit (CellarBayLamp), so the machines read before the lever, like the stair foot's. **Signage, in the fiction:** NACHLIEFERUNG painted on the arch's lintel (AUSGANG · KELLER on its bay side), a hanging enamel plate over the east aisle in line with the arch (NACHLIEFERUNG → / SKYHOOK SEKT · MAGPIE MEAD; the east wall itself is hidden behind the machines), the maker's name and a pinned delivery note between the machines (LIEFERSCHEIN 7b ... nicht bestellt), the crates' stencils (ZWEIGWERK THALBACH; RANGE SUPPLY · GROOM LAKE). PERKS_LORE.md section 4 gets both plates (machine_lore.json rebuilt): Skyhook came down the salt road from Werk Thalbach a month after the eight replacement units; Magpie, a Groom Lake range build and no Helvetia pattern, is "simply there" like the ninth cabinet. No real brands. **Skyhook on Map 0: kept as is.** Every effect is the drinker's own movement (air control, one mid-air hop, safe landings, faster and longer slides, push and pull resistance; tiers III/IV a landing stomp) and none needs Fernflug's flight: on Map 0 it is the hayloft's bale drop without the landing hit, slide escapes round the cellar's pillars and the grounds, and the resistances against the Lodestone's and the Undertow's pulls. The hop opens no new route: a mantle is measured from the floor you left (Player.probe_mantle's ref_y), so a hop reaches no ledge a jump didn't, and the fences' edge-layer tops (2.3 m) stand far above a two-hop apex (the feet reach about 1.1 m: a 0.63 m jump at 14 m/s² plus two 0.23 m hops). Prices (2500 / 3000), tiers and the cap of six are unchanged; Map 0 now pours twelve tonics for six slots. **Bots:** both are appended to economy.json perk_plan and perk_upgrades.plan and to the player agent's perk_order, after the nine: a bot buys them by rule when the earlier plan is drunk or missing and the cap has room, and brews their tiers when it holds them (an egg reward); in a plain match the cap is full first, which keeps T-177's balance. **A generator fix found on the way:** a `--script` run compiles gen_map00's dependencies before the autoload names are registered, so Landscape (GLSettings since T-139) failed to compile and the dressing pass lost the edge tree line (Landscape.uniform_tree_xform; the saved scene had no EdgeTrees); gen_map00._initialize now recompiles landscape.gd first. **Navmesh and minimap:** both bakes redone by the generator; every vertex of both outside the bay (and the field over it) is unchanged, compared vertex by vertex, so test_map00_art's two fingerprints were re-based. The minimap was re-baked under WSL + Xvfb (bake_minimap, software GL: the cellar floor shows the bay and its two machines) and its POIs refreshed (37); check_mapdata passes. Tests: test_map00_cellar_bay.gd (both machines in the bay and in zone CELLAR, needing power, prices and the cap, no footprint on another machine, the stair foot or the way to the arch, both bakes covering both fronts and the arch, the bay's lever bulb and lamp, the Map 0 plates, the bots' plan) and smoke_map00_cellar_bay (the live map: refused before the lever; navmesh paths to both fronts from the stair foot, the coal chute, the breach and the vault's middle; the player walks through the arch; the real Kellerschalter lights the bay's bulb and wakes both; the player's prompt at each and a held interact buys each for its price; Skyhook's hop and Magpie's drop chance live); test_gl_m2 counts twelve cellar machines. Results: suite 1870/1870, 760 scripts; smokes PASS: map00_cellar_bay, map00, map00_alive, map00_loop, m2 (one flamethrower flake, then PASS), bots, bots_shop_m0, bots_shop, jakub, bank, inn_survivors, inn_opening_paths, npc_walls, interact, cousins. smoke_marks fails its pause-map "an empty spot" check on master too (the click lands on a collectible; pre-existing, left alone).

T-181, 2026-11-06: the bots finish Kaltenbrunn's Requiem, the egg made easier, and Easter Egg Mode (the owner: "the Easter egg, they get stuck at the bell part. You can alter the Easter egg to make it easier for the bots to do. And ... make a mode where the zombies just don't spawn, or they spawn very minimally as crawlers"; branch u10-12-egg-easy). **Where they got stuck (master after T-176/T-177, headless soaks, EGG_DEBUG):** the Four Bells (s6): the bots walked to each bell's vantage 60-100 m apart and the 35 s "no shot" clock ran out on the walk itself, a vantage snapped against a boxcar's or the gatehouse's edge was never reached within 1.2 m (and at 0.8-1.6 m the bot kept stepping and never turned to fire), the aim wanted 1 degree, a burst's second round into the bell just rung reset all four, and a target's vantage cache (an INF "none found" too) outlived the task, so every later try was "unreachable", and one candidate (the gatehouse's) sat in the middle of a flat roof where no round from the ground ever reached it (30-70 shots a run); the Chapel bell (s4): the Foreman died at the Chapel's door or after the 90 s light went dark ("conditions_unmet" again and again while the squad held the Chapel at round 10, the most common honest wipe); the calling radios (s2a): a radio 12 m+ from every walked spot was never heard (no scout for "heard" needs), so s2b and s8a stayed locked; the shard (s8a): the use errand's 45 s clock ran on while the task was paused and its stand point sat against a Crucible-room prop ("couldn't use Crucible" every 20-30 s, T-172's 165); s8b: the Totenglocke bot held the heart while round 18's last straggler, which nobody else reached, kept the round open for 75 game minutes; s9: the agent (the player, who starts the Heartstorm) kept walking off in each break to a perk it had lost on a down while the squad waited at the heart. **The egg, easier for everyone** (still an egg: every step, its order and its inputs stay): a Four Bells input already rung this run is ignored, only an unrung bell out of order resets (s6 params repeat_ok, QuestManager.repeat_ignored); the gatehouse's candidate hangs from the roof's front lip over the WACHE sign (map01.tscn + gen_map01.gd; the other eleven spots are unchanged), a small bell's shot box is 1.1 m (was 0.64), a blast within 3 m rings it (QuestBell.splash from Explosion.detonate), and once the music box is open the four live bells glow faintly and chime their note every 20 s until s6 is done (map01.json requiem); the radios carry 18 m (was 12; QuestRadio.HEAR_M, s2a hear_m); the objective guides (objectives.json) and docs/gravelight/EASTER_EGGS.md say so; the Chapel bell stays lit 150 s solo / 120 s co-op (was 90 / 75) and the Foreman counts within 16 m of the altar as well as inside F_CHAPEL (s4 near / near_m, QuestManager.kill_zone_ok). **The bots:** the shoot walk has its own budget (30 s + 0.6 s a metre) and the 35 s stall counts at the vantage only; a clear line from where the bot stands (within 6 m of the vantage or 14 m of the target) is a vantage; at 1.6 m it stops and fires; the aim tolerance follows the target's size (1-4 degrees); the vantage cache is per task; the scout also walks for a collect step whose things are found by ear or eye (BotEEHub.collect_scout_due); a use errand's clock stops while paused and it acts from arm's reach when wedged (BotTasks.use_reach_ok); s8a pauses only at 0.85 pressure; a kill-weapon task joins a stalled round's hunt (pause_reason `hunt`); the egg's final step (the Heartstorm start) beats the agent's purchase gate and the trainer (BotEE.value, BotTrainer.value). Data: ee.json shoot_walk_s / shoot_walk_per_m / shoot_target_m / aim_tol_max_deg / shoot_here_m / shoot_here_near_vantage_m. **Result (honest: no god mode, three bots on Full Partner, the bot-mode agent as the player, ai_soak --scenario=botmode --map=map01 --bots=3 --ee=full --until-egg --speed=4, 150 game-minute cap; the code moved between batches):** master before this work: seed 42 wiped at round 14 (30.3 game min, s0-s5; at s6 the bot never reached a bell's vantage in time), god-mode seed 41 had s2a's third radio never heard by round 27 (s2b and s8a locked), god seed 43 from s6 did s6 and s7 and then never placed the shard. First batch (05574d2d-042f7932): 3 of 5 (seeds 71 / 72 / 75 in 39.0 / 38.1 / 36.8 game min, rounds 18-19, the first Requiems ever finished by bots; seed 73 failed: round 18 held open 75 game min by one straggler while the s8b bot held the heart; seed 74 stopped at 117 game min on s7). Second (8cdad74b): 2 of 5 (101 / 104 in 37.4 / 34.7; 102 and 105 wiped at rounds 12 and 10, 105 at the Chapel; 103 did s8b by round 22 and the agent never started the Heartstorm). Third (b4f0bffd): 2 of 5 (115 / 113 in 35.2 / 55.3; 111 wiped at round 10 in the Chapel after s4 failed conditions_unmet twice; 112 wiped in the Heartstorm at round 20; 114 hit the 150-minute cap on the gatehouse bell, 70 shots that never rang it). Fourth (2e3d46ed, the Chapel easing): 4 of 5 (121 / 122 / 123 / 124 in 31.6 / 31.3 / 42.3 / 51.6 game min, rounds 15-24; 125 stuck on the gatehouse bell, 44 shots, stopped at 98 game min). **Final code (66280431, the gatehouse bell moved): 3 of 5** (seeds 125 / 131 / 132 in 40.1 / 35.4 / 39.9 game min, rounds 18-19; 114 and 133 wiped in the Heartstorm, s9's 120 s holdout, at rounds 16 and 22 after s8b). **The target (3 of 5) is met on the final code, and 7 of the last 10 runs finished; what stops the rest is the Heartstorm holdout itself (wipes at rounds 16-22) and, less often, round 10-12 wipes.** The machine was shared with 5-8 other headless runs throughout. **Easter Egg Mode** (EggMode, rounds.json egg_mode; GLSettings egg_mode off / sparse / none): Settings > Gameplay "Easter Egg Mode (practice)" and a button on the map-select screen's bottom row (by "Back to survivors"); the host's setting, captured at match start (GameState.egg_mode). Few Crawlers: 2 crawlers a round (+1 every 10 rounds, 4 at most, never past the normal count), all crawlers with round-5 health at most; a round ends when they die or after 60 s (what is still queued is dropped). No Zombies: no queue, a round lasts 25 s; the normal 10-15 s break either way, so the round gates still open. What still comes: every boss and set piece an egg summons (the Foreman at the Chapel bell, der Kielholer, the Heartstorm's waves, the diesel holds' boarders: their own spawn paths, crawlers where they take an ordinary zombie), a few crawlers while a hold runs (3 in Few Crawlers, none in No Zombies) and, while a kill-count step is open, a feed of crawlers (6 + up to 4 left elsewhere, one every 2.5 s, at the step's own feed_points: Requiem s8b's by the heart). Off in it: Bellringer rounds, the scheduled Foreman, the Screamer and Glasshide rolls, homage boss rounds, relief squads. Every account gets 1250 Scrip at each round start (a couple of crawlers pay nothing and the egg's doors, pylons and Crucible still cost). Bots treat it calmly: no round-end pause and no straggler hunt (the round ends on its own clock). **The call on rewards: practice only.** No profile progress at all (XP, challenges, stats, best round, map unlocks, endings, egg records and the permanent egg rewards: ProfileSave.practice), the bank is shut (its savings live in the profile and the 40 s rounds would farm the 1% interest), the match's own in-game rewards (the egg's tonics and Scrip) still pay, and a small "EASTER EGG MODE · practice, no progress" tag (EggModeTag, its own layer) and a game-over line say so. Online: the host decides the spawns; a client's tag and practice flag follow its own setting (not mirrored yet). Measured: the bots alone finished the Requiem in No Zombies in 25.0 game min (seed 92, round 39, no downs) and in Few Crawlers in 73.7 game min (seed 106, round 65, no downs), and Totenhafen's Letzte Fahrt in No Zombies in 28.9 game min (seed 95, round 42). Normal mode reads none of it (EggMode.on() is false: every hook returns the old value). Tests: test_egg_mode.gd (11: the rules, the data, the crawler spec, the practice guard, the bank), test_requiem_easier.gd (7: repeat_ignored, the data, the marker, the walk budget, the aim tolerance, use_reach_ok, kill_zone_ok); smoke_egg_mode (Map 0 / Kaltenbrunn in Few Crawlers, Totenhafen in No Zombies: rounds advance and end, crawlers only, at most 4 up, no Bellringer round, the stipend, the bank shut, practice; the lit Chapel bell calls the Foreman, a pylon hold gets its few crawlers, s8b is fed by the heart, s6 of Letzte Fahrt brings der Kielholer; PASS); ai_soak --egg-mode=off|sparse|none.

T-185, 2026-11-06: the Kranich's launch into the sky, and a safety net on every map (the owner: "the map on the airplane has a glitch where it launches you in the sky. fix that. on all maps if a user gets too far away it respawns them back down"; branch u10-13-kranich-launch). **The cause:** Player.external_push (T-105: turbulence, suction, pulls, knock-backs) added its vector to the body's velocity on every physics frame of its seconds and never took it back out, so a push given in m/s acted as an acceleration of 60x that. Measured headless on master: the Bordwart's cable lash in the engine room (bordwart.gd _lash, 6 m/s away + 2 m/s up for 0.35 s) threw the player about 37 m/s upward, 56 m over the deck and through the roof (the Kranich's roofs have no collider): that is the launch. The same build-up made a 1.6 m/s tilt carry a standing player at 16 m/s, the bomb-bay doors' 5 m/s throw 30 m/s, a blown wing panel 25 m/s and a decompression 27 m/s; bots took the same push through the shared movement code (11.6 m/s in a tilt). Not the cause (probed, no launch): the tilt itself (camera and pushes only, no moving collider), the loose cargo (no collider), mantles, Skyhook's hop, jumps, and 240 pushes at the old 30 m/s across random points of the navmesh (no geometry turned them upward). Only the wind tunnel had worked round it (move_and_collide; left as it is, comment updated). **The fix, at the source:** Player.move_with_push: the push goes in for the move and comes back out after it, per axis by the share the collision let through (a free move gives the own velocity back exactly; a wall that stopped it leaves 0, never a bounce); used by the human's movement and BotMoveAdapter. A push's upward part is one take-off (velocity.y = max(own, lift), as Zombie._update_push already did): the lash is now a 2.1 m knock-back with a 0.15 m hop. Every other upward push gets the same: map04c Groom Lake's leap landing (+1.5) and trap door (+1.5), map04d's shaft grate (+3: about 90 m/s on master) and the Account Manager's arms (+2.5), map02's Kielholer knock. Push numbers and timings are unchanged; they now mean what the data says. **The safety net, BoundsGuard** (gravelight/world/bounds_guard.gd, attached by MapRoot on every map, fixed node name so online peers share its path): the host / solo checks every body in "players" (human, bots, companions, remote co-op humans) every check_s (0.25 s; one navmesh closest-point query per body, no raycasts). Rules: below (navmesh lowest point - below_m 8), above (navmesh top + a jump and a Skyhook hop at the map's gravity + the body, or a launch plate's arc apex, + ceiling_m 8), outside (the navmesh footprint grown by edge_m 15), falling (not on a floor and moving down longer than the fall from ceiling to kill height at the live gravity + 1.5 s), off_nav (on a still floor 5 m off the navmesh and outside every zone volume for 2 s: the Kranich's invisible deck plane outside the hull). Bounds come from every NavigationRegion3D (enabled or not, so the wing walk counts); defaults in world.json "play_bounds", any number (or kill_y / ceiling_y / max_fall_s / rect / enabled false) overridable in the map's json "play_bounds". Back to: the last safe floor (sampled each check while standing on the navmesh on a still floor), else the nearest navmesh point in an open zone, else the spawn; velocity, push and lunge zeroed, a 0.1 s dip to black and back for the local human, no damage, no down. Never while: not PLAYING, the "cutscene" flag (crash, rocket, endings), the dev console open, spectating, noclip, mantling, a launch plate's flight (Player.air_grace_t), standing on a moving floor (lifts), or held by a "play_space" node (WatchRoom: the Slender's quarantine at y -60); a downed body only from below / outside. Online the host decides and a remote human's rescue runs on its owner (_net_rescue via Net.mirror). BotLocomotion's T-131 free-fall rescue stands down when a guard is active (one net for every body). Each rescue prints "[bounds] P<n> <reason> at ...: back down at ... (<how>)" and counts in rescues / by_reason / BoundsGuard.rescues_total; console "bounds [up|out|down]". **Tests:** test_bounds_guard (push_removed cases; a renewed 5 m/s push never builds up and still moves the body 5 m/s; a lift is one take-off; derive with low gravity and pads; every verdict incl. downed; world.json defaults and overrides; a launched body put back on its last floor, still, unhurt; spectators and noclip left alone). smoke_bounds: map04b's tilt, bomb-bay doors, decompression, blown panel and the Bordwart's lash (FAILS on master: 15.9 / 30.9 / 24.9 m/s, rose 56.35 m; PASSES on the branch: 1.6 / 5.0 / 3.8 m/s, rose 0.16 m), then on map00, map01, map02, map04d and map04b the human over the ceiling, beyond the edge, under the floor (and on map04b on the deck outside the hull) and a bot in the sky each come back exactly once onto the navmesh, still and unhurt; map04d's three launch plates and low-g jumps rescue nothing; 90 s bot matches on map00 and map04b (rounds and tilts running) rescue nothing, top speed 6.7 / 6.4 m/s. Results: check_scripts 770/0, suite 1891/1891; smokes PASS with zero rescues in their logs: bounds, map04_egg, map04_flow, map04_full, map04a, map04a_world, map04b, map04b_world, map04c, map04c_world, map04d, map04d_world, sail, campaign, bots, bot_loco, coop, webrtc, turbulence, decompression, wing_walk, wind_tunnel, low_g, slender, kielholer.

T-186, 2026-11-06: the bots' round-30 kit (branch u10-13-bots-r30; the owner: "all bots spawn with all perks now after round 30 and a pack a punch weapon"). bots.json `r30_kit` {enabled, from_round 30, perks "all", exclude, weapon_tier 2, fallback_weapon thompson_up, on_revive false, companions false}; BotSquad.apply_r30_kit. **When:** every live squad bot when a round starts from round 30 (the round-25 hook's loop), and every squad bot that spawns, respawns after a bleed-out or joins at round 30+ (apply_kit, after the late kit), and a campaign arrival (add_brain's carried path; Map 4's Act IV starts at round 30, and the carry's restore runs later, so the next round start is the safety net). **What:** every tonic in perks.json it doesn't hold ("all", the same set as the Requiem's grant_all_perks), granted quietly (PerkManager.grant's new `quiet`: no perk_bought, so no twelve drink lines, drink poses and machine flourishes at once; perks_changed still updates the HUD, the nameplates and the co-op snapshot) and outside the cap like the Requiem's grant (grant never checks the cap, only buying does), at the tier it already has (new ones tier I: the tiers stay the bots' own buys, and tier IV would make them permanent). And its main gun taught to at least tier 2 (BotSquad.r30_gun_plan: its best long gun by economy tier, a taught gun counting its teachings; never a pistol or a wonder, as T-163's "a gun before a wonder"): an untaught gun becomes its taught form in its slot with its attachments and finish, full, in hand; one taught once gets its second teaching; with no long gun that can be taught it gets the fallback, the Chicago Lament II (a free slot, else the weakest). Tier 2 = the late kit's tier: a doubled gun; the third teaching (10,000) stays theirs to buy, so the Crucible errand and the re-teach saving (T-121) still mean something. **Calls:** (1) It stacks on the round-25 late kit (that runs first: its tier III tonic and the Chicago Lament II; the round-30 kit adds the other tonics and teaches a better gun the bot taught itself, e.g. an MG42 taught once, which out-rates the Lament II). With the late kit on, a bot's main gun at 30 is usually the Lament II already. (2) Downs: the tonics are not permanent (a down loses them like any other, the bonus perk and sealed / tier IV ones excepted); a bleed-out respawn gives them all back at once; a revive does not (on_revive false: a down still costs a bot the rest of the round), the next round start does. (3) The Bot Bonus Perk setting gates it, as it gates the late kit (the same "bot help" toggle), and Classic Rules turns it off. (4) Companions (Jakub, Trudi, Gustl, Hinnerk, Jette) don't get it: the owner names "the extra bots and npcs you find" apart (T-177), the companions have no spawn kit by design, shop for themselves since T-177 and take 0.6 damage on a balance that assumed few tonics; `companions` true turns it on for them (round starts and joins). Never the bot-mode agent (it plays the human's survivor; it has no brain in `brains` and isn't is_bot) or a dropped online player's stand-in (brain option kit false: the human's own kit stays as it was). (5) No new VO: the LATE_KIT lines name round 25 and the Thompson, so the round-30 kit is silent (new lines would need a bake). **Economy (T-177) after it:** a bot holding everything buys no tonic (can_buy "owned"; perk_wanted finds nothing, so the tier brews take over); the Crucible's re-teaching works on the taught gun as before. One fix: the bank's "next thing it wants" (BotEconomy.next_want_cost) now includes the next tier the bot would brew (with nothing new to drink, upgrade_wanted's pick at any price, plus its extra_reserve), between the next tonic and the Crucible; before, a bot with the cap full (and so every bot after round 30) banked everything above its reserve every break and could never save up for a tier (upgrade_wanted's "an unaffordable first choice is saved for" was undone by the deposit). With no want at all the deposit keeps the reserve and the re-teach saving, as before. Tests: test_bot_r30_kit (the gate and toggles, the data, "all", the main-gun plan, the quiet grant, every tonic past the cap and lost on a down); smoke_late_kit extended (round 29 nothing new; round 30 every live bot holds all 12 tonics and a gun taught to 2 in hand with its late tonic still at III; idempotent; an MG42 taught once is taught to II in place; a bleed-out respawn at 31 comes back with all of it; a pistol-only bot gets the fallback Lament II; a revive at 31 waits, round 32 brings them back; the toggle off gives nothing). Results: suite 1890/1890, 768 scripts; smokes PASS: late_kit, bots, bots_shop, bots_shop_m0, jakub, map_companions.

T-187, 2026-11-07: two fixes found while merging T-185 / T-186. An enemy row may give its kill pay as one number ("scrip": 60, the U10 Totenhafen enemies) instead of per hit zone ({head, neck, body, melee}): Zombie._die passed the number to Economy.kill_value, which expects a Dictionary, so every such kill raised a SCRIPT ERROR and paid nothing (smoke_bots_u10 logged 9); a number now pays as a flat kill value. smoke_bounds' bot-match check ("nobody moved faster than a sprint and a push", 12 m/s) measured speed from one frame to the next, so a bot's one-frame snap of ~0.27 m (a stair step, a wall sweep) read as 16 m/s once on merged master; it now measures over 0.1 s windows (a real launch keeps its speed; the per-push checks still use the per-frame speed) and prints who was fastest.

T-184, 2026-11-06: the cutscenes read like film (the owner: "make the cut scenes better and more cinematic"; branch u10-12-cutscenes). **The inventory** (length before -> after): the campaign hops on ExfilCutscene, data in campaign.json `cutscenes`: Map 0 -> 1 KALTENBRUNN 30 -> 30 s, Map 1 -> 2 TOTENHAFEN 30 -> 30, Map 2 -> Act I WERK THALBACH 24 -> 26, Act I -> II DER KRANICH 38 -> 38, Act II -> III GROOM LAKE 40 -> 40, Act III -> IV MONDLICHT 40 -> 40; Totenhafen's egg dive LETZTE FAHRT 34 + the 6 s telegram card (unchanged); Kaltenbrunn's Requiem ending 40 -> 40 (it was "cutscene-lite" in the players' own eyes, now a real cutscene); the London window 50 -> 50; Fernflug's endings KÜNDIGUNG 162 -> 162 and AUTO-RENEWAL 40 -> 40. The Inn opening (T-072/T-076) stays in the player's own eyes (taking the camera at round 1 would change play) but gets the film's first-shot treatment over its settled view: the letterbox, the HUD stepping aside, the title card GASTHAUS ZUM KREUZWEG / the Crossroads Inn · 9 km northwest of Kaltenbrunn / 23 December 1944 · night (map00_opening.json `title_card`, the same words as the map's intro banner, which it covers), all gone at 8.2 s or at the cue, whichever is first; the player keeps control and every timing. Left as they are, on purpose: the homage bosses' and set-pieces' entrances (BossHud title cards during live rounds), the loading card. **The shared library** (gravelight/ui/cinema/): CinemaEase (named curves; an unknown name falls back to smootherstep, so nothing moves like a lerp), CinemaShot (one shot from data: dolly / crane with `arc` / orbit by `sweep` or from->to / static; look and look_to pans with their own curve; fov -> fov_to; lead and hold; `rel` offsets from a moving node (a tracking shot); `target` keeps a node in frame (the Kranich taking off, the rocket climbing, the U-boat diving); roll; focus and blur; a grade; cut / blend / dip in; shake kicks; the events vo, vo2, sfx, sfx3d, stinger, caption, light (a motivated lamp for the shot), squad), CinemaCamera (samples the shot every frame, blends from the last frame on "blend", a seeded hand-held drift (layered slow sines, under 0.6 degrees) and impact trauma (squared, decays in under a second), both scaled by the Screen Shake setting so 0 is a locked-off camera; depth of field through CameraAttributesPractical on Forward+ only, never on the Compatibility / browser renderer or the low preset), CinemaOverlay (2.39:1 letterbox, never under 8.5 % of the height, sliding in and out; the film fade and the dip; the grade as one canvas shader over the 3D picture (exposure, contrast, saturation, tint, lift, vignette; presets night, dawn, warm, cold, storm, salt, violet, desert, space, memory), so it reads the same on Forward+ and the Compatibility renderer and costs one screen copy; the title card: the place in the stamp face, a violet rule drawn out from the middle, the place / date / time typed in the typewriter face with the stamp and teletype sounds; narration captions above the bottom bar; "HOLD [key] TO SKIP" for keyboard and pad (Input.is_action_pressed(interact), a fill bar, a fresh press only so the key still held from the beacon's vote never skips); the HUD hidden: every CanvasLayer under the map (HUD, minimap, the bots' nameplates; not the PostFx film look), the world marks' labels (WorldMarks) and the perk row, put back on release or exit), CinemaCast (the squad as actors: the local player has no body of their own and the bots' brains are paused, so the free camera used to find a floating gun and frozen figures; now every standing squad member, Jakub and the companions included, is played by a stand-in RemoteBody (their own PersonRig, face and gun, name tags off), the real bodies hidden (RemoteBody.cinema_hidden, so a co-op puppet stays hidden too) and the viewmodel off; a shot can `place` them in a loose wedge behind the cut, turn them to `face` a point and `walk` them; idle fidgets run; nothing moves a Player, a collider or anything the network sends), CinemaDirector (the timeline: shots on the clock, their events once each at shot-local times, the title, the fade, the music (stop, an opening stinger, an ambience bed that swells in and out), `end` black or return; script-driven `play_shot` for the endings; `step(dt)` is the whole update so tests drive it; scaled by Campaign.cutscene_speed; `missing` lists target / rel names the map lacks; `validate(def, lines)` checks a definition without its map). **What changed in each**: the exfil hops were re-shot (the stable's great doors falling outward seen over the squad's shoulders, the reverse low in the snow as they walk out into the lens, a crane up over the crossroads at first light, the long dolly after them down the east road; the minehead gate grinding up, the snow line looking back through it, alongside them on the north road, the crane up with the headframe black against the dawn; the bow apron by U-1047, the catwalk pan along her, the long lens from the pen mouth; the ramp, the bomb bay as it shuts, the hangar doors, the take-off tracked on a long lens; the cockpit and Peg with the airframe's kicks, the crash followed in, the wreck at first light through a dip; the gantry, the capsule, the launch tracked from the rim, the climb, and a Moon at the end (fx `moon`: the range's sky had none, the last shot looked at an empty sky); the dive with the U-boat tracked as she goes down); every title card split into place / where / when; ExfilCutscene keeps its API (cutscene_id, exfil, refresh_skip, stop, _t, the fx names the tests read) and its rules (every peer plays its own copy, the host's ends it, a skip is the co-op vote through ExfilPoint.request_skip_local, nothing else changed in the net code); with no exfil it emits `skipped` / `ended` for a quest runner. The Requiem ending: the heart from under its cage, the collapse from a pillar with the flash and the kick, the squad's faces as the lights blow out, an orbit round them in the dark (the Operator's tape), the Ledger as the power comes back, the rail yard at dawn (the train heard: whistle and steam; the old banner as a caption), the crane up from the squad; input waits for it (it used to leave the players walking blind); a skip still lands every world effect (the heart, the dead, the lights) before the step completes. Letzte Fahrt: a clock instead of loose timers, hold to skip goes straight to the dive (flag and signal as ever) and the telegram, now typed onto the paper with a second hold to dismiss it. The London window: the lens pushes from the blister to the battery's plumes, tilts down onto the city with a kick per burst, holds in the silence, pulls back on the squad's lines; input waits; hold to skip now on the first view too (ProfileSave still records seen_london_window). KÜNDIGUNG: the insert on the key and the two-shot of the key-turner with Jakub at their side, the low wide under the sign as the columns go dark, the Manager from below her station, from the squad's backs up to the Earth, each stock shot a dip into a slow push in the drained "memory" grade, the cards typed on black, the squad low from the front for the count and the last word, the credits as the camera tilts up to the Earth over the game's theme (music_menu: there is no music_fernflug yet); hold to skip always (it was only after a first viewing; the work order says always skippable). AUTO-RENEWAL stays in the player's own eyes (the match runs; the HUD stays; skip is off there because [interact] is the buy key) except one 8 s insert of the night side going violet from the yard (input waits; hold to cut back), the violet grade, the card in the title-card treatment. Flags, rewards, step completion, the profile, the next map's load and every smoke's hooks are unchanged; lengths are within the old ones +/- 30 % (test_cinema checks). Subtitles: VOManager's Subtitles has `cinema(on, bar_px)`, the lines sit in the bottom bar during a cutscene (same timing, same style). No new VO and no new assets: every sound is from audio.json. **Verify**: gravelight/tools_src/clip_cutscene.tscn plays any of the twelve (the eleven and `inn_opening`) the game's way (`--cut=`; `--stills=` for a quick look; `--skip-at=`; fails on a missing node) and tools/wsl_cutscene_clip.sh films it through WSL + Xvfb (software GL, invisible) with a contact sheet and a still per second; before / after clips in morning/clips/t184_before and t184_after (git-ignored). Tests: test_cinema (curves, shot timing and paths, lead/hold, crane, orbit, rel/target, kicks, drift bounds, blend, the Screen Shake off switch, the letterbox height, the grade presets, hold-to-skip, the director's clock and speed, manual endings, the wedge, every cutscene validates, every target / rel / fx node exists in its map's scene, the lengths). **Measured**: GUT 1908/1908 (test_cinema 24), check_scripts 775/0; smokes PASS: campaign, transitions, map04_flow, map04_full, map04b, map04c, map04d, map04b/c/d_world, sail, lf_start, inn_opening_paths, inn_survivors, map00, act_bosses, requiem, launcher, bots_u10, map04_egg, ee_bots, jakub, kielholer, earthrise, coop (smoke_letzte_fahrt reached s9 and hit the runner's 25-min cap, as on master); every cutscene headless through clip_cutscene: PASS, no missing nodes. A fix found by watching: the overlay's black was anchored after add_child and stayed 0 x 0, so no fade or dip drew (test_cinema now checks it covers the frame). **Needs the owner's GPU**: depth of field (Forward+ only; WSL films the Compatibility renderer) and how the grade sits on the Forward+ tonemapper; the clips are software-rendered at 960x540.

T-188, 2026-11-07: the perk machines and the Crucible collide with what you see (the owner: "fix the perks"; T-180's open item). Both stood in plain boxes from their scripts (perk_machine.gd: the shape's footprint, 2.4 m tall over a rounded cabinet top; crucible.gd: 2.0 x 2.4 x 1.6 m) that reached 20-30 cm past the art, and on the maps the generators stood each machine in a NavBlock box bigger still (Fernflug I's NavBlock_Perk_* walls: 6-80 rays each in MapArtAudit). ArtCollider (gravelight/world/art_collider.gd): fit() gives the body one convex hull per connected piece of each art mesh in the owner's frame (pieces(): triangles sharing a corner, to 1 mm; a merged body mesh holding the cabinet, its sign arch and its feet as one hull bridged the air between the wide sign and the narrower cabinet), pieces under 8 cm left out, the old box kept when there is no art; free_blocks_under() (MapRoot, deferred to after the loaders) turns a NavBlock_* box that stands under a perk machine, the Crucible, the Armorer's Bench, a crate spot or the bank counter navmesh-only (layer 0 + bake_only), the way T-180 did Totenhafen's by name; the baked navmesh is untouched, so no zombie or bot path moves, and the use boxes, prices, prompts and collision layers are unchanged. MapArtAudit after: no perk machine finding on any of the eight maps (Fernflug I had five NavBlock_Perk walls); the Crucible from 73 rays to 6 on Totenhafen and 3-22 elsewhere (its model's body is one concave piece; a convex decomposition would close the rest). Left: Map 0's cellar-bay crate collider (BayCrateCol, a 1 m box under a 0.5 m top crate: 7 rays; needs a gen_map00 regeneration), and the Fernflug acts' other NavBlock walls the audit lists (gun racks, the radio, the relay, the generator shed, the Moon's capsule, rack, edge, conveyor and Crucible bay: 5-1256 rays each), not perks. Tests: test_art_collider (pieces, the fit's height, the fallback); test_world_objects_visual now checks the cabinet body is fitted and no taller than the art, and leaves fitted bodies out of its procedural-vs-model collider comparison (the use areas must still match).

T-189, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 "LAZARUS" starts (docs/work_orders/UPDATE_11_LAZARUS.md; contracts docs/U11_CONTRACTS.md). The work order names Fable 5.1 as the lead; this session runs on Opus 5.5, so the lead stays Opus and every story-writing task (the canon, the shot lists, the VO rewrite, the respect review) goes to a Fable worker, as the work order's table asks. The egg guard was taken on master before any change (f1a8e737): gravelight/tools_src/egg_guard.gd hashes every quest file (and each of its steps and top-level keys; doc keys starting with "_" left out) into gravelight/tests/fixtures/u11_egg_guard.json, and test_u11_egg_guard fails on any difference. ElevenLabs: the owner ran the CLI onboarding at the start of this session; the key lives in the main checkout's git-ignored .env.local (the CLI added it to .gitignore), never in the environment of a commit or a log; the pipeline loads it at run time (C8). The VO rewrite is split by speaker, not by map (C4): the lines are tabled by speaker in VO_SCRIPT.md and CHARACTERS.md, so a per-speaker slice keeps one voice consistent and the rows of two slices never touch; the "thins out map by map" rule is applied within each slice by the line's map. lines.json conflicts are resolved by re-running extract_vo.py over the merged docs.

T-191, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 §5 the shot lists and every cutscene line (branch u11-5-shots; docs/CUTSCENES_LAZARUS.md, VO_SCRIPT.md §26). **The shot lists** (34 + 12 + 10 + 11 shots and the three-card epilogue, each with number, duration, set, the lens and move in the cinema library's own terms, what is seen, sound, line ids, grade and the cards' exact text) and, per cutscene, the sets with what comes from the existing kit and what is new, the cast and their period costumes, and build notes for T-194. The calls: (1) **Dates and period**: Orthodox Pascha 1930 was 20 April (Gregorian), so Lazarus Saturday is 12 April 1930 and "the fourth day" 16 April; the brigade's truck is an AMO-F-15 (1924-31), not the GAZ-AA the work order suggested (production from 1932); the OGPU is the OGPU (the NKVD's GUGB only from July 1934); Volkov in 1944 wears a state-security colonel's cap (cornflower crown, maroon band), the Red Army officers 1943 shoulder boards under rain capes; the grave markers' tin stars are a period detail shown with respect, never framed as a symbol; the brigade's slogans BRG_LZS_03/_04 are the League of Militant Godless's real ones, read flat off a paper. (2) **No violence on screen in 1930** is staged as cuts: the villagers are pulled off the road out of focus while the camera holds on Serafim, Serafim is taken from the door on a 0.4 s dip to black with the sound carrying it, and only aftermath is seen (a torn cuff, the keys on the boards, a shawl in the mud); the bell's crack and the tower's fall are the only destruction shown. Serafim is never struck, never falls, never bleeds and is never a zombie on screen (his fate stays T-190's diary line). (3) **The cards are Latin only**: the stamp face (Stardos Stencil) and the typewriter face (Special Elite) have no Cyrillic glyphs (checked with fontTools: 0 of U+0400..U+04FF; Godot's has_char says true only through the system-font fallback), so Chetverodnevo is transliterated on every card and Cyrillic appears only as painted text on a prop (the typhus board). The subtitle face (IBM Plex Sans) has Cyrillic, but the subtitle is always the English `text`. (4) **The crypt's wet blood is gore-gated** (full: red with a running drop; low: red, no drop; off: dark and wet), as are the sheet's stain and the mound's; nothing else in the four cutscenes is gore. (5) **The prologue runs 224 s**, not 180: the three cards (the opening, "The fourth day.", "Chetverodnevo is not on any map.") and the aftermath movement (ward, cordon, train, the empty road) need the time; the work order says "about 3 minutes" and T-194 may trim shots 29-32 if it must. The epilogue is cards only, unvoiced (contract C3 prefers it; NAR_EPI53_01 is reserved if a cut wants a voice).
